## Document 003: 10月企業向けサービス価格指数 ～前年比＋4.2％、国際運輸以外でも伸びが拡大～

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月企業向けサービス価格指数 ～前年比＋4.2％、国際運輸以外でも伸びが拡大～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-25
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026年 11月 25日

1/2 
10月企業向けサービス価格指数 
～前年比＋4.2％、国際運輸以外でも伸びが拡大～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  阿原 健一郎 
（℡：050-5470-4868）

1. 10月の企業向けサービス価格指数は前年比＋4.2％に加速 
 本日公表された 10月の企業向けサービス価格指数は前年比＋4.2％と、前月（同＋3.8％）から
伸びが加速した。内訳を見ると、「運輸・郵便」の前年比寄与度が＋1.53％ptとなり、引き続き総
平均を押し上げている（図表１）。なかでも「外航貨物輸送」は前年比＋120.4％と、前月（同＋
97.6％）から上昇幅が一段と拡大した。 
 また、「広告」の下落幅が前年比▲1.2％と、前月（同▲5.3％）から縮小したことも、総平均の
伸びを押し上げた。特にテレビ・ラジオ広告の下落幅縮小が大きく、広告全体では総平均の前年
比の伸びを前月から＋0.18％pt押し上げている。このほか、宿泊サービスや廃棄物処理、自動車
整備で上昇幅が拡大し、「諸サービス」も前年比＋3.1％と、前月（同＋2.8％）から伸びが高まっ
た。今月の加速には、国際運輸の価格上昇に加え、国内サービスの価格変動も寄与している。

２. 国際運輸を除いても伸びが拡大、高人件費率サービスは底堅く推移 
 海外市況の影響を受けやすい国際運輸を除いた「企業向けサービス価格指数（除く国際運輸）」
は前年比＋3.2％と、前月（同＋3.0％）から伸びが高まった（図表２）。外航貨物輸送や広告など
を含む「低人件費率サービス」は同＋5.9％と、前月（同＋5.0％）から伸びが大きく拡大してお
り、総平均の加速を主導している。 
 一方、人件費の影響を受けやすい「高人件費率サービス」は前年比＋2.7％と、前月（同＋
2.6％）から小幅に伸びが高まった。前年比＋２％台後半の上昇が続いており、人件費増加を価格
に転嫁する動きは維持されているとみられる。もっとも、労働者派遣サービスなどで伸びが高ま
る一方、ソフトウェア開発や警備などでは鈍化しており、品目ごとの動きにはばらつきがある。
総平均の加速に比べて高人件費率サービスの伸びの拡大は小幅にとどまっていることから、賃金
を起点とする価格上昇が一段と強まったとまでは評価しにくい。今後も、人件費の価格転嫁が幅
広い分野で継続し、サービス価格の持続的な上昇につながるかを注視する必要がある。

以 上

AI 資本支出侵蝕現金流，科技巨頭財報拖累科技股 
                                   2026 年 10 月 30 日 
一、 市場動態 
1. AI 資本支出與現金流疑慮再起，那斯達克領跌：美股 10 月 29 日主要指數收黑，S&P 
500 下跌 0.30%、Nasdaq 下跌 1.38%、道瓊下跌 0.25%、費半下跌 1.42%，費半 ETF
盤後跌幅進一步擴大至逾 3%。市場下跌主要因 Alphabet 財報營收與獲利低於預期、
搜尋廣告業務成長放緩，加上大幅增加資本支出導致單季自由現金流轉負，股價重挫
12.64%，再度引發市場對 AI 投資報酬的疑慮，賣壓亦擴及半導體及軟體類股。其他
盤後公布財報的科技公司表現亦出現雜音，Amazon 資本支出明顯高於預期，近 12
個月自由現金流轉為淨流出，Intel 則因 PC 需求疲弱下修財測，使科技股盤後賣壓進
一步擴大。不過，當日 S&P 500 約 81%成分股上漲，顯示市場賣壓主要集中於 AI 相
關大型科技股，而非整體市場同步走弱。 
2. Fed 按兵不動但兩票主張升息，12 月升息機率仍高：Fed 於 10 月 28 日以 10 比 2 維
持利率於 3.75%～4.00%，Cook 與 Hammack 主張升息 1 碼，期貨市場隱含 12 月
升息機率約 85%。9 月 PCE 年增 3.6%，10 年期美債殖利率在 5%附近震盪。 
3. 中東情勢緊張，油價維持百元以上高檔：布蘭特原油 10 月 28 日升至每桶 115 美元附
近，WTI 亦站上 102 美元。高油價增加通膨與 Fed 升息壓力，也推升 AI 資料中心電
力成本，持續壓抑科技股評價。

二、 大型科技企業財報重點： 
1. 雲端業務全面加速，AI 需求仍未降溫：本週公布財報的大型科技企業中，雲端業務成
長普遍加速。Alphabet 旗下 Google Cloud 營收 307.09 億美元、年增 103%，TPU
系統銷售成為重要動能；Microsoft Azure 年增 48%，下季財測進一步加速至 49～
50%；Amazon AWS 營收 459.8 億美元、年增 39.3%，為近五年最快增速。Intel 資
料中心與 AI 營收亦年增 78%，創 15 年來最強成長。各家均表示算力需求仍高於可用
供給，顯示 AI 基礎建設需求尚未出現降溫跡象。 
2. 資本支出持續攀升，自由現金流表現決定股價分歧：Microsoft 於 10 月 28 日盤後公
布 2027 會計年度第一季財報，營收 922.79 億美元、年增 18.8%，營業利益率達
49.8%，自由現金流亦維持正值，整體財報優於市場預期。相較之下，Alphabet 雲端
業務表現亮眼，但廣告業務成長未達預期，且單季資本支出達 513.55 億美元，導致
自由現金流轉為負 90.65 億美元，股價於 10 月 29 日重挫 12.64%；Amazon 單季資
本支出則達 773 億美元，近 12 個月自由現金流轉為淨流出 424 億美元，加上第四季
營收財測中值低於預期，盤後股價下跌逾 6%。資本支出指引方面，Alphabet 維持 2026
年 1,950 億至 2,050 億美元，Amazon 維持約 2,200 億美元，Microsoft 則因第三方

三、 市場看法：
1. 市場不再單純獎勵 AI 投資，開始要求可驗證的財務報酬：Microsoft 與 Alphabet 同

樣大舉投資 AI，股價反應卻明顯分歧，關鍵在於 AI 投資是否已轉化為獲利與現金流。
我們認為，市場評價標準已由「是否投資 AI」轉向「AI 投資能否帶來足夠的營收成長
與現金流」，具備穩定現金流與明確商業化能力的企業將相對受青睞，而自由現金流持
續為負、高度依賴外部融資的業者則可能面臨評價壓縮。

2. 雲端未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求尚未出現結構性反轉：Microsoft 商業 RPO 達 6,770
億美元、年增 73%，Oracle RPO 約 6,640 億美元，AWS 與 Google Cloud backlog
亦分別達 5,780 億及 5,610 億美元，四家合計約 2.48 兆美元（詳見圖 3）。這顯示目
前市場疑慮主要在於投資回收期與資金壓力，而非終端需求惡化；只要雲端業者持續
擴充算力，先進製程、伺服器與資料中心供應鏈中期仍具支撐。
3. 資本支出 2027 年仍將成長，台灣 AI 供應鏈持續受惠：四大雲端／平台業者 2026 年

資本支出合計約 7,200～7,400 億美元，且 2027 年仍將增加。元大投顧認為，Google
擴大 TPU 對外銷售及 Microsoft 持續部署 GPU 與自研 Maia 加速器，將帶動伺服器
ODM、散熱及機殼需求，受惠供應鏈包括廣達、奇鋐、雙鴻、健策、富世達及聯發科。
融資面則宜留意 AI 相關企業公司債利差及再融資條件變化。
4. Fed 鷹派立場限制評價空間，短線震盪可能延續：12 月升息預期升溫、通膨與油價維
持高檔，使 10 年期美債殖利率停留在 5%附近，壓抑高評價科技股評價擴張。只要
AI 需求未出現結構性反轉，維持短線審慎，對科技股中期偏正向看法。
圖 3：主要雲端業者未認列合約（Backlog／RPO）規模（單位：億美元）

四、市場展望：
1. 科技股短線波動較大，中期看好現金流穩健的 AI 受惠企業：本週財報顯示雲端營收加速
成長與未認列合約持續擴大，AI 需求仍強；但資本支出攀升、自由現金流轉弱，加上 Fed
可能於 12 月升息，短線科技股波動恐將延續。中期而言，AI 產業將由全面成長轉向基本
面分化，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健且 AI 投資已能轉化為獲利的企業，如雲端與 AI 軟體
平台領先者，以及先進製程、伺服器、散熱等關鍵供應鏈，風險報酬相對有利；對高度依
賴外部融資、自由現金流持續為負的企業則宜維持審慎，並留意零組件成本上升對 PC 等
消費性電子需求的排擠效應。
2. 美債避險功能仍在，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：10 月 29 日科技股下跌時，10 年期

美債殖利率由約 5.05%回落至約 4.99%，顯示美債仍具一定避險效果。惟 Fed 鷹派立場
及油價高檔使短天期公債易受升息預期影響，超長天期公債則面臨財政赤字與期限溢價壓
力，中天期公債風險報酬相對均衡。投資級債中期維持偏正向；高收益債則須留意 AI 相
關發行人自由現金流轉負及利差擴大風險。

3. 下週財報焦點：Apple、Palantir 與 AMD 接棒，檢驗 AI 需求延續性：下週（11/2～11/6）
大型科技財報持續登場。Apple 及 Palantir 將於 11 月 2 日（美東時間）盤後公布，市場
將關注 iPhone 新機需求、記憶體成本對毛利率的影響，以及 AI 軟體商業化進度；AMD
與 Arista Networks 將於 11 月 3 日盤後公布，AMD 將提供 AI 加速器（Instinct GPU）
與伺服器 CPU 需求的最新證據，Arista 則可觀察資料中心網路設備需求。在本週雲端業

者確認資本支出持續成長後，下週財報將進一步檢驗 AI 供應鏈訂單能否轉化為營收與獲
利（詳見圖 4）。

assist with process execution, which we think should shorten the learning curve. Nevertheless, depreciation is
expected to rise sharply from approximately NT$9.1bn in 2026 to NT$14–16bn in 2027. As a result, consolidated GM
could fall from above 32% in 2026 to roughly 25–30% in 2027, with gradual improvement throughout 2027 as
utilization improves. The established 8-inch business could reach the mid-30% range, while the new 12-inch may

remain at a 20–30% GM during its depreciation period.
• Market turbulence, recovery path and risks. VIS believes AI-related power-management demand remains intact
despite concerns about offshore computing restrictions for Chinese AI, project financing and possible changes in
GPU deployment. Power content rises with increasing compute density, faster signal transitions and more complex
voltage-management requirements across GPUs, ASICs, networking and cooling systems. For traditional consumer

electronics market, where high memory prices are raising notebook and smartphone costs and delaying replacement
demand. VIS has partly protected itself by cutting consumer capacity by more than 20% and reallocating it to AI and
server products. Other risks include higher substrate, chemical, gas, electricity and labor costs, as well as slower
Singapore qualification. Longer term GM should recover toward or above 30% in 2028–29 if the new fab reaches

targeted utilization, but the pace will depend on interposer volumes, IC yields and customer ramp schedules.

Vanguard International Semi.
Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$160 is based on 20x 2027E P/E, which we see as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth
momentum and increasing outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash dividend policy. Our
target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.8x/3.7x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along with higher costs due to inflation or more severe

ASP competition from Chinese peers; 2) lower UTR of the new capacity at the newly acquired fab, with depreciation burden;
and 3) less-than-expected outsourcing opportunities from IDMs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

達興材料(5234)：半導體材料轉型效益逐步顯現 
建議：維持買進

一、公司積極朝半導體特化廠轉
型 
 達興材料成立於 2006 年，過去為國內最大面板化學品供應商，近年則積極朝半
導體材料發展。營運總部及研發中心位於台中中科，而台中中科、中港各有一座
先進材料製造中心；桃園、蘇州、深圳設有業務據點。9M26 產品組合中，顯示
器材料佔營收 75%、半導體材料 23%，關鍵原材料則為 2.0%。銷售區域來看，
台灣、中國分別佔營收 67%及 32%，其他國家約 1%。

二、4Q26 獲利率重返上升軌道  受惠半導體材料營運增長，3Q26 營收季增 7.8%至 13.82 億元，但顯示器材料
來料異常而認列跌價損失 3,000 萬元，導致毛利率略為滑落至 41.3%，單季 EPS
為 2.29 元(+2.7%QoQ, +17.0%YoY)；累計前三季 EPS 為 6.73 元(+26.1%YoY)。
展望 4Q26，半導體彌補顯示器營收缺口，管理層預期營收持平至小幅季增，在
原料成本漲勢趨緩且無跌價損失下，毛利率將重回上升趨勢，預估單季 EPS 為
2.63 元(+15.1%QoQ, +19.5%YoY)。公司近期公告 Oct-26 自結獲利，營益率較
3Q26 的 19.9%提高至 21.9%。

三、半導體材料挹注中長期營運
動能 
 隨新產能持續投入且生產效率提高，累計前三季半導體營收成長 60.3%至 8.96
億元，其中，先進製程與先進封裝應用貢獻各半。此外，3Q26 半導體材料營收
比重達 27.1%，大幅年增 8.1 個百分點，管理層預期 4Q26 佔比將進一步擴大
至 30%左右，目標在 2030 年比重達到 50%。目前半導體材料量產品項為 13
支，包括 7 支自製產品、6 支與國際大廠策略合作生產，2H26 有 5 支產品上線
測試，而驗證中品項則有 14 支。目前中港二廠 1 樓的 10 條線已滿載，2027 年
將投入 2 樓的 10 條線，帶動營收維持雙位數增幅。公司為支應客戶對半導體材
料的長期需求，投入 22 億元興建橋科新廠，預計 2028 年完工、2029 年開始貢
獻。

四、顯示器藉產品組合維持獲利
表現 
 2027 年台灣顯示器客戶有做產能削減規劃，但中國客戶還是有新產品需求。另
一方面，公司新設立江蘇子公司，主要配合中國顯示器客戶的材料在地化需求，
也透過該公司持續開拓顯示器的客源。中長期營運方向也包括優化生產製程、提
高原料自製比例，保持 PS & Cu 蝕刻液優勢並提升市佔率，也同步擴大液晶配
向膜 PSA PI 導入新量產廠區。

五、維持買進理由  受惠半導體材料動能持續成長，加上顯示器材料產品組合優化，預估 2026、2027
年 EPS 為 9.36 及 11.97 元。隨中港廠完成生產空間擴充，最大產能由 24 條擴
增至 40 條線且生產效率提升，而橋頭新廠則預計 1Q27 動工、2028 年完工，

## Document 225: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

### Page 8

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

표 5. 삼성전자 이론적 적정 주가는 465,690원 
투자 지표 내용 비고

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 290: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-26)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-26).pdf
Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-26
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings have generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than two-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.”2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market.2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of investment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractive in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market moves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting government
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

### Page 1

DeepSeek⽹⻚端和App端悄然上线Token⽤量账单，输⼊、输出消耗及对应费⽤⼀⽬了
然，但⽬前聊天仍免费。叠加此前免费额度收紧、算⼒不⾜及调⽤量激增等情况，引发外
界对收费计划的猜测。与此同时，⽹⻚版限额⼜疑似取消，新模型预期再度升温。
摘要：
DeepSeek开始给你算账了。
眼尖的⼩伙伴们突然发现，DeepSeek⽹⻚和App端的回复下⽅，悄咪咪地多了⼀个形似⼀摞硬
币的⼩图标。
点开⼀看，好家伙，刚才跟DeepSeek聊的这⼏句值多少钱，全给你算明⽩了。
本轮消耗额度、输⼊（缓存命中/未命中）及输出Token的单价、⽤量、⾦额，全都列得清清楚
楚。
最下边甚⾄还配了个上下⽂占⽤进度圈，连聊到现在占⽤了多少上下⽂空间都能⼀眼看到。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

不过⽬前账单归账单，聊天依旧免费。
结合前阵⼦DeepSeek给免费⽤⼾限额冲上热搜的事，不少⼩伙伴已经开始猜测，⼤肥⻥是不是
已经在为收费做准备了？
有⼈调侃，梁⽂锋这波操作，是想让前阵⼦因为限额⽽骂他的免费⽤⼾们，亲眼看看⾃⼰到底
消耗了多少算⼒。（doge）
Token账单直接搬进了聊天框
看到消息后，我第⼀时间跑去实测了⼀波，发现⼤肥⻥⽹⻚和App端确实都已经上线了这个功
能。
在⼿机上开启深度思考，⽤粤语和DeepSeek打了个招呼：
它秒回了⼀个：
这⼀来⼀回，花了0.000426元。
再翻出⽹⻚端⼀轮输⼊超1.3万Token的对话，账单显⽰约0.0088元，也才不到⼀分钱。
你好咩？
你好呀！有咩可以帮到你?

### Page 3

顺着账单仔细⼀扒后我发现，这套计价规则咱们其实并不陌⽣，与DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash的API
空闲时段定价正好对得上。
按照空闲时段的价格，每百万Token输⼊（缓存命中）仅需0.02元、输⼊（缓存未命中）需1
元，输出则需4元。
⾼峰时段的价格统⼀翻倍。
DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash API价格卡（系AI⽣图）
也就是说，以前只有API开发者才接触得到的按量计费，现在被⼤肥⻥原封不动地搬进了普通⽤
⼾的聊天框⾥。
有⽹友已经开始畅想，以后免费额度⽤完了，是不是直接充点钱就能接着聊了？
不过⽬前看来，⼤肥⻥似乎还处在计费功能的试⽔阶段，账单已经算得明明⽩⽩，收费⽅案却
尚未官宣，聊天也依旧免费。（难道是想让咱们先有个⼼理准备？）

### Page 4

⼤肥⻥最近确实有点⼉缺算⼒
话说，就在⼗来天前（11⽉16号），#DeepSeek使⽤额度已达上限#才刚登上热搜。
当时，不少⼩伙伴聊得正起劲呢，突然就被DeepSeek下了逐客令：
每天免费聊天有额度了，⽤完就得等恢复；
单次⽂字输⼊上限被压缩到了4万字左右；
就连超过72⼩时不活跃的⻓对话，也没法接着往下聊了。
对于⻓对话受限，DeepSeek在系统提⽰中给出的理由也相当直接：
偏偏这段时间，⼤肥⻥的Token调⽤量还在猛猛往上涨。

Token，环⽐暴涨801%。
图源OpenRouter
到了今天，这⼀数字更是飙到了59.2万亿Token，稳居平台调⽤量榜⾸。
算⼒不⾜。

### Page 5

这场限额⻛波，甚⾄还⼀度引爆了业界对国产AI算⼒供给缺⼝的讨论。

摊到了所有⽤⼾⾯前。
种种迹象都表明，⼤肥⻥真的要开始精打细算地过⽇⼦了。
⾄于接下来究竟会不会收费、⼜会怎么收，就等梁圣亲⾃揭晓答案了。

就在⼤家纷纷猜测DeepSeek是不是要开始收费时，还有⼩伙伴惊喜地发现，今天⽹⻚版的限额
似乎悄⽆声息地消失了。

## Document 332: First Impression_ RBNZ survey of inflation expectations, December quarter 2026 (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impression_ RBNZ survey of inflation expectations, December quarter 2026 (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#inflation #newzealand
First Impression: RBNZ survey of inflation expectations,
December quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
13:50 November 17 2026 Share
Inflation expectations remained contained in the RBNZ’s latest survey. High oil prices and
elevated near-term inflation don’t appear to have boosted medium-term inflation
expectations.
RBNZ inflation expectations survey

### Page 2

1 year ahead: 2.92% (Prev: 2.60%, up 32bps)
2 years ahead: 2.36% (Prev: 2.34%, up 2bps)
5 years ahead: 2.17% (Prev: 2.31%, down 14bps)
10 years ahead: 2.13% (Prev: 2.20%, down 7bp)

There was only limited movement in the RBNZ’s latest survey of inflation expectations. 
Importantly for the RBNZ’s policy deliberations ahead of their December policy meeting, the detail of
today’s update points to inflation expectations remaining well contained despite the current oil-related
lift in inflation.

Looking at the details of the December inflation expectations update:
Expectations for inflation 1 year ahead did rise to 2.92% (vs 2.60% last quarter). That’s consistent with
the sharp rise in fuel prices in recent months, which we expect will push overall inflation up to 4.3% by
the end of this year. 
However, expectations for inflation at longer horizons were much more stable. Notably, the closely
watched measure of inflation in two years’ time nudged just slightly higher, rising to 2.36% (vs 2.34%
previously). 
Similarly, expectations for inflation at long horizons (5 and 10 years ahead) actually slipped back this
quarter and now sit just a little above 2%.

Implications for the RBNZ
Today’s result will be welcome news for the RBNZ. With oil prices pushing inflation to high levels, there
was a risk that expectations for inflation would take a step higher, adding to the pressure on prices over
the year ahead. Such risks had been alluded to in a recent speech by the RBNZ’s Chief Economist.
Nevertheless, the RBNZ is still looking at an elevated near-term inflation outlook. Importantly, the
strength in inflation isn’t just due to high oil prices. Core inflation has remained at firm levels for an
extended period. 
Consistent with those firm underlying inflation pressures, we continue to expect an OCR hike at the
RBNZ’s upcoming 9 December policy meeting. 
We’ll discuss our thoughts in more detail in our latest Economic Overview, out tomorrow.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

## Document 334: First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#retail #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ retail trade, September quarter 2026 - New
Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:19 November 23 2026 Share
The September quarter saw a larger than expected rise in spending, including firmness in
core categories.
September quarter retail sales
Retail sales (volume of goods sold): +2.2% (Prev: -0.4%)

### Page 2

Westpac f/c: 1.4%, Market: +0.5% 
Core retail sales (volume of goods sold): 1.4% (Prev: 0.8%)

Nominal retail sales: 2.4% (Prev: 1.0%)

Year to September
Volume of goods sold: 3.5%
Nominal sales: 7.0%

New Zealanders fill up the car and head back to the mall.
The September quarter retail sales report showed a sharp recovery in spending. Nominal spending
was up 2.4% over the quarter, and the amount of goods that New Zealanders are actually taking home
was up a solid 2.2%.
Today’s result was above our top of market forecast for a 1.4% quarterly rise, and well above the
median market forecast for +0.5%.
While the strong September quarter result is welcome news, we are taking it with a grain of salt. Much
of the rise in spending was related to fuel spending, which was boosted by the fall in fuel prices over
the September quarter, and fuel prices have step higher since then.
However, even excluding fuel, we have seen firmness in spending appetites. Core spending (excl. fuel
and motor vehicles) was up 1.4% over the September quarter and is 4.7% higher than this time last
year. 
Looking at the breakdown of spending, we did see a rise in grocery purchases. Meanwhile, spending in
discretionary categories was mixed – there has been lower spending on items like furnishings and
hardware, while spending on hospitality, electronics and recreational items is up. Overall, were left with
a picture of gradually improving spending appetites.

Outlook
Today’s report reinforces our expectations that the economy is moving into stronger phase. We expect
that spending levels will continue to firm through the holiday season and 2027. However, that recovery
is still likely to be gradual due to the combination of ongoing high inflation, a soft labour market and
rising interest rates. We also expect the recovery will be uneven, with those regions with strong links to
the agricultural and tourism sectors outperforming metro regions, like Auckland and Wellington.

Implications for GDP growth
We’re forecasting GDP growth of 0.6% in the September quarter. Today’s result was above
expectations. We’ll take a closer look at how our forecast for GDP growth is shaping up over the next
couple of weeks as additional data on September quarter activity is released.

Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist

透明度降，加劇長天期公債利率上行風險，

產，盈利貢獻有限。Model 3/Y成為大眾商
品，競爭激烈，利潤率壓縮。正如傳統品牌
以賽道或參賽展示技術，「飛天」旨在展示
獨有跨界整合能力，維持品牌形象溢價。

Terafab的開支等，現時兩家合計市值約為
3.5兆美元。這項巨型潛在交易，除了要跨過
極高表決門檻外，SpaceX具極敏感的美國國
防及NASA合作背景，而特斯拉有超過20%
業務倚重上海超級工廠，合併存在嚴重地緣
衝突。預期馬斯克將在川普離任前，加速執
行Tesla與SpaceX 合併。

► 今年9月適逢地緣擾動持續推升高油價、聯準
會重啟 升息 、財政部與企 業發債 浪潮 等影
響，形塑債市今年以來單月最差表現。檢視
2021年以來美國投資級債前十大單月最差總
報酬，2022年聯準會3月啟動升息年度就佔
一半以上，顯示利率風險仍為近5年來債市最
大風險，然檢視單月最差報酬表現未來期間
總報酬，除2022年1月和3月聯準會重啟升息
前夕與當月表現明顯較弱，多數於未來1年後
多呈現正報酬，反映隨利率走升放大孳息效
益，同時挹注債券長期持有之報酬空間。
► 今年來債券市場利率波動明顯推升信用債利
差，其 中僅 以低 信 評債利 差升幅 較顯 ，此
外，9月以來利率連動度高及AI相關產業債種
也成為利差擴大重災區，凸顯債市投資人減
輕市場不確定性的投資傾向，考量信用債市
歷經利率上行利空洗禮，信用利差同步擴大

創造收益空間，可把握債信體質仍佳企業債
鎖利良機，並透過時間複利力量強化收益。
利率波動升，僅低信評利差擴大程度相對明顯9月為美國投資級債今年最差單月報酬
用時間換空間，單月最差表現形塑未來報酬機會

► 受聯準會重啟升息影響，過去一年美國公債
殖利率曲線持續陡峭化，其中利率最大升幅
落在短天期的2至3年期間，美國核心通膨與
就業仍具韌性，聯準會將持續保持緊縮貨幣
政策步調來應對高通膨，考量美國高物價與
高利率環境短期難消，宜透過縮短債券配置
天期並進行動態調整，為因應後續利率不確
定性環境的良策。
► 為因應利率不確定性，投資人可採取債券梯
的投資配置，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，債券梯投資重點主要在
於，根據殖利率曲線型態及利率動態建立不
同期間債券梯，假設市場利率走升且殖利率
曲線正斜率，建立短天期債券梯除了降低利
率風險，有 短期 閒 置資金 及孳息 需求 投資
人，亦可取得穩定孳息收益。若後續投資人
有短期資金需求，可以視情況將短期債券到
期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至
到期或直接變現的流動性風險。
投資焦點
美國公債殖利率曲線持續為正斜率
高利率波動持續，用策略迎戰市場擾動
資料來源：Bloomberg，凱基證券整理
建立短天期債券梯，分散風險及穩定現金流

債券梯
經過第2年第一檔債券到期，視資金需求及市況與利率動
態決定後續配置

短天期債券梯：假設買入2檔各3、4、5及7年到期債券，
聚焦短期穩定收益
► 債券梯是一種分散投資方式，主要選取不同到期期間及不同
類型債劵進行組合，可兼具：
1) 分散利率風險：將資金分別買進不同到期年期的債券，以分
散利率風險。
2) 分散信用風險：分散投資不同發行機構債券，不會重壓單一
債券並可分散信用違約風險。
3) 降低流動性風險：若投資人有短期資金需求，可以視情況將
短期債券到期後資金回收，降低只投資單一債券須持至到期
或直接變現之流動性風險。

### Page 9

9
股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注

### Page 18

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

半反彈幅度最大，反映短線市場恐慌情緒有
所緩解。然而，AI疑慮並未完全消散，儘管
德州州長當選人Gina Hinojosa表示並非全面
反對資料中心開發，但資料中心監管趨嚴的
疑慮仍在市場迴盪。另一方面，10月CPI數
據高於預期，搭配優於預期的非農數據，支
撐了市場對於聯準會12月升息1碼之預估，

短線股市反彈能否延續所需觀察的變數。
► 產業方面，CoreWeave財報與展望優於市場
預期，並進一步說明積壓訂單、客戶承諾與
融資規畫，帶動半導體、伺服器、記憶體及
資料中心相關個股買盤。然而，輝達則受執
行長 黃 仁 勳遭 到 調 查且 宣 布 請辭 之 消 息影

勢。其餘產業則是呈現上漲，但醫療保健、
必需消費等防禦型產業在恐慌情緒放緩下多
數漲幅落後於成長型產業。

及墨西哥灣新離岸油田陸續投產，則可望帶
動美國原油產量持續成長。

6
投資焦點
► 近期美股市場風格加速轉換，隨著市場更加
重視AI商業化成效與投資回報，高估值成長
股波動顯著升溫。相較之下，價值股則逐漸
受到市場青睞，尤其是憑藉穩定現金流、健
康資產負債表與較高股東回報率之高品質價
值股，除了擁有價值優勢之外，更具備更好
防禦力與抗震能力。
► 從風險維度進行觀察，近10年股市面臨3次
升息循環，不論是2016年、2022年甚至是
2026年9月啟動之升息，高品質價值股指數
之90天波動度平均值皆低於成長股與大盤指
數，顯示若將具備穩定獲利、自由現金流與
較低財 務槓 桿之 高 品質價 值股納 入投 資組
合，有助降低市場修正期間的波動風險。
► 長期而言，高品質價值股的累積報酬率雖不
及成長股在景氣擴張期的爆發力，但仍具優
於大盤的累積報酬潛力，代表在市場波動加
劇、利率升高的環境下，其兼具防禦與長期
複利之優勢已然是長期投資不可或缺關鍵。
高品質價值股長期報酬優於大盤價值股相對成長股獲市場青睞，波動度亦相對偏低
成長股短線波動大，留意價值股表現機會

### Page 7

7
投資焦點國防股
► 國防股通常具備穩定攀升之自由現金流，主
要受惠於長期政府合約與產業高進入門檻所
支持。相較景氣循環股，國防產業獲利與現
金流波動幅度較低，使其兼具一定程度的防
禦性與長期價值配置優勢。
► 北美國防企業近年受惠於地緣政治風險升溫
與軍備補庫存需求，訂單能見度明顯提升。
包含飛彈、防空系統、軍機及潛艦等關鍵裝
備，有助維持歷史高檔積壓訂單水位。龐大
訂單庫存不僅支撐未來數年營收成長，也有
助降低短期景氣波動對企業基本面的影響。
► 根據SIPRI 資料，2025 年 全 球 軍 事 支 出 達
2.89兆美元，已連續11年成長，全球軍事支
出占GDP比重升至2.5%，創2009年以來新
高。其中歐洲軍費年增14%、亞洲與大洋洲
年增 8.1%。在美伊衝突、區域安全風險及北
約提高國防支出目標等因素帶動下，各國軍
事現代化需求可望延續，皆將為國防產業提
供長期且具結構性的成長動能。
地緣政治問題未解，全球軍事支出持續增加國防產業現金流穩定攀升，積壓訂單成長強勁
現金流穩定，補庫存預期支撐後續國防股走勢

### Page 8

8
股市
 黃仁勳相關事件恐逐步強化美國對AI晶片出口管制、AI資料中心建設進程或將受到州政府緊縮政策
而造成評價面壓力並增添股價波動。短期可留意防禦股以增強抗震力以抵禦市場震盪。長期而言，
AI從全面看好轉向選股，且大優於小，市場波瀾過後產業長期正向趨勢未變。當前則可布局財務健
全度較高的高品質價值股，以獲取穩定報酬並降低市場波動影響。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好轉向選股、且大優於小，市場波
瀾後長期正向趨勢未變，當前可布局財務
健全度較高的高品質價值股
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 美伊談判走向反覆未明，油價維持高檔震盪。10月CPI高於預期，強化市場對聯準會升息步伐預估，
聯準會官員立場亦偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率
走升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具
吸引力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。

債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 10月CPI數據支撐升息預期，美元走勢偏強。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理
商品
 美伊談判走向反覆，油價一度回落但再度反彈，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 373: Monitoring Romania_ A recovery is taking shape, but the margin for error remains narrow

Source PDF: all report pdf/Monitoring Romania_ A recovery is taking shape, but the margin for error remains narrow.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Romania enters 2027 at an economic handover point. The RRF
sprint is over, and the next phase of growth should increasingly
rely on recovering real incomes, continued investments and
the addition of new energy and industrial capacity. But the
recovery will remain vulnerable to ﬁscal execution and global
ﬁnancial conditions

Stefan Posea and Valentin Tataru
Bucharest, Romania. We expect the economy to grow by 2.2% in 2027 and
2.4% in 2028
Romania at a glance
Growth: We retain our forecast of a 0.5% contraction in 2026,
followed by 2.2% growth in 2027. A very strong third quarter
(+2.1% versus the previous quarter) nevertheless creates upside
potential, with something closer to stagnation now
increasingly plausible this year.
Fiscal policy: The 2027 budget will have to accommodate
cautious pension increases and possibly higher public wages

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.

### Page 2

while keeping the deﬁcit on a declining path. The internal and
external constraints point towards little room for error ahead.
EU funds: The RRF has ended, although it remains to be seen
whether it ended in glory. Public oﬃcials have pointed to
around €3bn potentially being at risk.
Energy and external balance: The start of gas production from
the Neptun Deep oﬀshore project in the Black Sea, alongside
potentially lower international energy prices, should gradually

reduce Romania’s import dependence and improve its terms of
trade. However, with production only beginning and ramping
up during 2027, the impact that year is unlikely to be
particularly large.
Growth: contraction might be avoided
Romania’s economy has spent almost three years close to
stagnation. Private consumption contracted in 2026 under the
combined pressure of high inﬂation, higher taxes and frozen public
wages and pensions, while industry and parts of the services sector

remained weak. Construction, boosted by EU-funded investment,
provided the main buﬀer and prevented a deeper downturn. This
has broadly been the story throughout the past year.
The ﬂash estimate for third-quarter GDP has materially improved
the starting point. Output expanded by 2.1% QoQ, well above
expectations, while annual growth returned marginally to positive
territory.
We are still waiting for the detailed breakdown, but construction
remains a prime suspect behind the upside surprise, given the ﬁnal

RRF implementation push. Agriculture and inventories may also
have contributed positively. If the second estimate due on 7
December remains broadly aligned with the ﬂash reading, full-year
growth closer to stagnation rather than our current -0.5% forecast
could become the more likely outcome.
For 2027 and 2028, we retain our 2.2% and 2.4% growth forecasts.
While the rebound should indeed rely partly on recovering private
consumption as real incomes start rising again, we do not expect a

full return to the consumption-led growth model of previous years.

### Page 3

Instead, 2027 and 2028 increasingly look like a return towards the
country’s newer speed limit, rather than a return to the growth
rates of the previous decade. That is consistent with our broader
assessment that potential growth has fallen to around 2.0% today,
with scope to recover towards 2.4%-2.7% over 2027-2029.
Public investment will probably moderate after the end of the RRF,
although it should remain robust. In terms of composition,
cohesion funds, SAFE and the early stages of a growing pipeline of

private projects should partly oﬀset the RRF wind-down. Better and
still-expanding transport infrastructure could also start attracting
complementary private investment in logistics, manufacturing and
distribution, lifting productive capacity.
This is also where one of the risks lies. The problem is not the end of
the RRF itself, but a potential lack of continuity in projects, ﬁnancing
and administrative execution. Combined with a slower recovery in

household consumption, that could leave the rebound looking
considerably weaker.
Households: a modest real income recovery
rather than a new spending boom
Private consumption should once again become the largest
contributor to growth in 2027, although we expect the recovery to
unfold gradually. Recent retail sales dynamics have been
consistent with recessionary conditions, as households entered
2026 after two consecutive years without regular public pension

and wage indexation, while elevated inﬂation substantially eroded
purchasing power.

### Page 4

Source: NSI, ING
The outlook should gradually improve as real incomes start rising
again. The government has committed to raising pensions in 2027
after two years of freezes, although the ﬁnal adjustment remains
undecided. Public discussion has centred on an increase of around
7.0%, possibly implemented in stages during the year.
For consumption, timing matters almost as much as the headline
increase. An earlier adjustment would support household spending

more quickly, while a phased increase would imply a more gradual
recovery in real purchasing power. Even so, higher pensions and
potentially higher public wages should provide only a limited boost
to consumption, acting mainly as a partial oﬀset for the freezes of
the previous two years.
A broader recovery will depend more heavily on disinﬂation,
resilient employment and a gradual rebuilding of conﬁdence. Lower
energy costs and productivity gains will therefore remain important

variables to watch.
Fiscal policy: improvement has been
material, but 2027 remains difﬁcult
Romania’s ﬁscal adjustment has so far advanced faster than
initially expected. The cash deﬁcit stood at roughly 3.8% of GDP in
the ﬁrst nine months of 2026, compared with 5.3% a year earlier,
supported by stronger VAT receipts, reﬂecting both tax increases
and improved collection, alongside tighter control of public wages
and social spending. Importantly, EU-ﬁnanced investment

### Page 5

remained elevated, avoiding the broad cuts to capital spending
that have accompanied previous consolidation episodes.
We continue to see the full-year cash deﬁcit at around 6.0% of GDP
in 2026 and roughly 5.1% in 2027.
Pensions are the most visible new budget commitment. Published
estimates put the full-year cost of a 7.3% increase at around RON9-
10bn, or roughly 0.50% of GDP. A phased adjustment cannot be
excluded, as this would limit the near-term pressure on an already

demanding ﬁscal path. At the time of writing, neither the pension
decision nor the 2027 budget has been ﬁnalised, with both likely to
remain December decisions.
Overall, the room for manoeuvre remains constrained by interest
expenditure of more than 3.0% of GDP, defence and energy-security
commitments, and the need to preserve public investment at
robust, albeit probably declining, levels.
Rating outlook
At this stage, with ﬁscal adjustment progressing and a government

with full powers in oﬃce, we think the probability of a downgrade
has diminished. Romania is not out of the woods yet, though.
Throughout 2026, rating agency guidance has placed emphasis on
the credibility and continuity of ﬁscal consolidation rather than
simply the adjustment already delivered. In our view, an inside
target deﬁcit outcome this year, followed by another visible
improvement in early 2027, could make a return to a stable outlook
increasingly plausible by mid-2027. A more predictable political

and policy environment would also help. After a year in which
political uncertainty blurred the improving ﬁscal story, preserving
continuity around the consolidation path will probably matter
almost as much as the headline deﬁcit numbers themselves.
The margin for error therefore remains narrow. Expenditure
slippage, or renewed uncertainty around the policy direction, could
quickly translate into higher yields, renewed pressure on the leu
and, eventually, renewed downgrade concerns.

### Page 6

EU funds: RRF ends, but let’s not call it a
cliff yet
Romania’s public investment will naturally ease in 2027 from its
2026 RRF-related peak. The ﬁnal RRF push was broad-based,
spanning transport, energy, healthcare, education, water and
waste infrastructure and residential energy eﬃciency. At the same
time, serious absorption concerns remain around roughly €3bn of
the €6.4bn still outstanding.
From 2027, stronger cohesion-policy absorption could partly oﬀset

the end of the RRF. Overall, Romania could absorb around €8-10bn
in EU funds next year, including €4.5-5.5bn from cohesion funds,
with the remainder coming from agriculture, rural development and
other transfers, plus SAFE.
The macroeconomic eﬀect should gradually shift away from the
direct demand impulse towards productivity gains from
infrastructure already delivered: shorter transport times, better
connectivity, additional energy capacity and stronger incentives for

private investment.
SAFE could provide another important bridge. Romania has a
sizeable €16.68bn loan envelope, of which €2.5bn, or 15%, has
been disbursed as pre-ﬁnancing so far. The programme includes
€4.2bn for dual-use sections of the A7 and A8 motorways,
alongside sizeable defence contracts, some involving domestic
production. With the ﬁrst implementation report and payment
request expected in the ﬁrst half of 2027, Romania could
potentially draw another €2-3bn next year, provided procurement

and construction remain broadly on schedule.
Industry: the external environment is
becoming somewhat more supportive
Romanian industry remains in a prolonged downturn. Industrial
production fell by 3.7% YoY in the ﬁrst nine months of 2026, while
manufacturing declined by 4.7%. Weak German and broader
European demand, ﬁnancing conditions and structural
competitiveness problems have all contributed.

### Page 7

The external picture for 2027 is unlikely to impress, but it should at
least become less negative. Our baseline assumes growth of 1.2%
in the eurozone, 1.1% in Germany, 2.3% in the US and 4.5% in
China. Recent European indicators also point to a cautiously ﬁrmer
near-term picture. Germany’s November Ifo index rose for a
seventh consecutive month to 90.6, its highest level in more than
three years, supporting the view that the cyclical recovery is

continuing as ﬁscal stimulus feeds into activity. Unbreakable,
German business conﬁdence continues to improve. Germany’s
infrastructure and defence spending should therefore provide some
support to European industry, although the spillovers to Romania
may prove weaker than in previous German upswings.
For Romania, the most relevant channels run through automotive
components, electrical equipment, machinery, metalworking,
construction materials, logistics, IT, engineering and defence-

related manufacturing. Lower energy costs would particularly
beneﬁt chemicals, fertilisers, metals, glass, ceramics, paper and
food processing.
In any case, stronger German demand cannot solve Romania’s
productivity, skills or energy-cost challenges. But it could reduce
pressure on industrial employment and provide incentives to renew
ageing capital stock. The real upside would come from Romanian
companies integrating more deeply into European defence,

infrastructure and energy supply chains rather than simply
beneﬁting from a cyclical recovery in orders. So far, we have yet to
see systematic evidence of this.
External balance: improvement should
become less dependent on weak demand
Romania’s current account adjustment has so far been surprisingly
modest given domestic demand weakness. Fiscal tightening has
clearly weighed on consumption, but an investment cycle that
remains intensive in imported machinery, equipment and raw

materials continues to obscure part of the improvement in
household-related imports. At the same time, higher interest

### Page 1

Copper and gold continued to recover on Friday as risk appetite
improved, while oil extended its advance amid renewed
concerns over Middle East supply disruptions. Zinc also moved
higher after the planned suspension of production at a
European smelter

Ewa Manthey and Warren Patterson
Monthly US copper imports surged to a 12-year high of more than 200,000
tonnes in July ahead of potential tariﬀs
Energy - Middle East risks lift oil prices
ICE Brent extended gains for a fourth consecutive session on Friday,
trading above $97/bbl and heading for its ﬁrst weekly gain this
month. Prices remain supported by attacks on energy infrastructure
and shipping in the Middle East. The latest disruption involved
Saudi Arabia's 400k b/d Jazan reﬁnery, following earlier attacks on
the Ras Tanura export hub and Red Sea ports, adding uncertainty
around the recovery in regional crude exports.
Attention now turns to Sunday's OPEC+ meeting. The group is
expected to consider keeping current production quotas unchanged

through January, although some members have been pushing for
higher quotas. Meanwhile, Saudi Aramco is expected to cut the
January Arab Light OSP to Asia, after setting December barrels at a
$7/bbl discount to the Oman-Dubai benchmark.
In reﬁned products, inventories moved in opposite directions across
the main trading hubs. ARA stocks fell by 280kt WoW to 4.2mt, led
by lower gasoil and naphtha inventories. Singapore stocks,
meanwhile, increased by 949k barrels to 40.9m barrels, driven by

higher light distillate and residual fuel stocks, while middle
distillates edged lower.
Metals - Copper rebounds as risk appetite
improves
Copper extended its recovery on Friday, with LME prices climbing
back above $12,600/t after falling below $11,500/t earlier in the
week. The rebound came alongside stronger global equities as risk
appetite improved following several volatile sessions.
The move appears to have been ampliﬁed by short covering, while

the physical market in China remains relatively ﬁrm. The Yangshan
premium has eased from last week’s record levels of $500/t but
remains elevated at around $320/t, while the spread between
Chinese spot prices and international benchmarks continues to
point to a premium for metal in China.
Focus will remain on Chinese demand and inventories, as well as
the potential movement of US-held copper back into the global
market.
In other metals, zinc prices jumped, with LME 3-month futures

gaining more than 3% yesterday after Nyrstar announced plans to
suspend zinc production at its Budel smelter in the Netherlands.
The 300ktpa facility has been under strategic review since
September, with high European energy costs continuing to weigh
on smelter margins. The suspension has added to concerns over
reﬁned zinc availability, while highlighting the competitive
pressures facing European smelters as Chinese production expands.
